        Idea:
# Sprint 6: Production Selector and Claim Contract

## Overall Context

This sprint turns local selector ideas into production claim behavior before orchestrated routes depend on them. It is the control-plane foundation for rollback and canary.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Production missing selector key means WGP/no-claim, never implicit Comfy.
- Workers must not claim routes they cannot execute.
- Selector version and selected backend must be visible in logs or task metadata.
- Child rows created later must be able to snapshot route selection.

## Sprint Goal

Make selector and claim behavior concrete for production.

## Required Deliverables

- Selector schema/namespace.
- Route-key serialization, including direct variants where needed.
- Index/RPC/query behavior.
- Cache TTL and rollback SLO.
- Malformed/unauthorized/stale-entry tests.
- Claim-time backend eligibility or pre-execution requeue/fail-closed guard.
- Selector-version logging.
- Child-route snapshot field contract for later parent-created rows.

## Exit Criteria

Missing production route key means WGP/no-claim; mismatched workers cannot claim or execute selected routes; selector unreachable behavior and rollback SLO are tested; selected backend/selector version can be pinned for child rows created after parent claim.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the direct adapter still has a correctness risk around resolution parity. current vibecomfy ready templates hardcode image dimensions (`image/z_image` uses `emptysd3latentimage` 1024x1024 and `image/qwen_image_2512` uses 768x768), while `vibecomfy run` exposes no `--resolution` flag; the revised plan says a temporary scratchpad/input file may be generated only if supported, otherwise resolution is left out. that means a vibecomfy-supported direct route can silently ignore the worker's `resolution` param unless scratchpad resolution patching is made mandatory for supported direct routes or the support is explicitly limited to default-resolution smoke fixtures. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: the attached success criteria are stale and do not match the current plan body. they still require 'no `usestate` for `activepairdata`', '`setactivepairdata` fully removed', 'sync effect deleted', and '`onpairclick` simplified to `(pairindex: number) => void`', which are the opposite of the current targeted-sync plan. as presented, the criteria are not usable for review or execution. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue. EXISTS tasks_claimed_route_key_check,\n+  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS tasks_selected_backend_check,\n+  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS tasks_claimed_backend_check,\n+  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS tasks_selector_version_check,\n+  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS tasks_claimed_selector_version_check,\n+  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS tasks_claimed_capability_version_check,\n+  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS tasks_route_selection_snapshot_object_check,\n+  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS tasks_claim_decision_snapshot_object_check,\n+  ADD CONSTRAINT tasks_selector_namespace_check\n+    CHECK (selector_namespace IS NULL OR selector_namespace ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}$'),\n+  ADD CONSTRAINT tasks_claimed_selector_namespace_check\n+    CHECK (claimed_selector_namespace IS NULL OR claimed_selector_namespace ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}$'),\n+  ADD CONSTRAINT tasks_route_key_check\n+    CHECK (route_key IS NULL OR (length(route_key) BETWEEN 1 AND 512 AND route_key !~ '\\s')),\n+  ADD CONSTRAINT tasks_claimed_route_key_check\n+    CHECK (claimed_route_key IS NULL OR (length(claimed_route_key) BETWEEN 1 AND 512 AND claimed_route_key !~ '\\s')),\n+  ADD CONSTRAINT tasks_selected_backend_check\n+    CHECK (selected_backend IS NULL OR selected_backend IN ('wgp', 'vibecomfy')),\n+  ADD CONSTRAINT tasks_claimed_backend_check\n+    CHECK (claimed_backend IS NULL OR claimed_backend IN ('wgp', 'vibecomfy')),\n+  ADD CONSTRAINT tasks_selector_version_check\n+    CHECK (selector_version IS NULL OR selector_version > 0),\n+  ADD CONSTRAINT tasks_claimed_selector_version_check\n+    CHECK (claimed_selector_version IS NULL OR claimed_selector_version > 0),\n+  ADD CONSTRAINT tasks_claimed_capability_version_check\n+    CHECK (claimed_capability_version IS NULL OR claimed_capability_version > 0),\n+  ADD CONSTRAINT tasks_route_selection_snapshot_object_check\n+    CHECK (route_selection_snapshot IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(route_selection_snapshot) = 'object'),\n+  ADD CONSTRAINT tasks_claim_decision_snapshot_object_check\n+    CHECK (claim_decision_snapshot IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(claim_decision_snapshot) = 'object');\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tasks_route_key_queued\n+  ON public.tasks (route_key, created_at)\n+  WHERE status = 'Queued'::task_status AND route_key IS NOT NULL;\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tasks_selected_backend_queued\n+  ON public.tasks (selected_backend, created_at)\n+  WHERE status = 'Queued'::task_status AND selected_backend IS NOT NULL;\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tasks_claimed_backend_active\n+  ON public.tasks (claimed_backend, claimed_selector_namespace, updated_at)\n+  WHERE status = 'In Progress'::task_status AND claimed_backend IS NOT NULL;\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tasks_claimed_route_key_active\n+  ON public.tasks (claimed_route_key, updated_at)\n+  WHERE status = 'In Progress'::task_status AND claimed_route_key IS NOT NULL;\n+\n+CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.route_backend_claim_decision(\n+  p_selector_namespace text,\n+  p_route_key text,\n+  p_worker_backend text,\n+  p_now timestamptz DEFAULT now()\n+)\n+RETURNS TABLE(\n+  selector_namespace text,\n+  route_key text,\n+  worker_backend text,\n+  selected_backend text,\n+  selector_version bigint,\n+  selector_present boolean,\n+  selector_enabled boolean,\n+  selector_expired boolean,\n+  capability_present boolean,\n+  capability_version bigint,\n+  capability_supports_route boolean,\n+  capability_supports_missing_selector boolean,\n+  eligible boolean,\n+  decision_reason text,\n+  selector_snapshot jsonb,\n+  capability_snapshot jsonb\n+)\n+LANGUAGE sql\n+STABLE\n+SECURITY DEFINER\n+SET search_path = public\n+AS $$\n+  WITH normalized AS (\n+    SELECT\n+      COALESCE(NULLIF(p_selector_namespace, ''), 'production') AS selector_namespace,\n+      p_route_key AS route_key,\n+      p_worker_backend AS worker_backend,\n+      p_now AS decision_at\n+  ),\n+  selector_row AS (\n+    SELECT s.*\n+    FROM public.route_backend_selectors s\n+    JOIN normalized n\n+      ON s.selector_namespace = n.selector_namespace\n+     AND s.route_key = n.route_key\n+    LIMIT 1\n+  ),\n+  capability_row AS (\n+    SELECT c.*\n+    FROM public.route_backend_capabilities c\n+    JOIN normalized n\n+      ON c.backend = n.worker_backend\n+     AND c.route_key = n.route_key\n+    LIMIT 1\n+  )\n+  SELECT\n+    n.selector_namespace,\n+    n.route_key,\n+    n.worker_backend,\n+    s.selected_backend,\n+    s.selector_version,\n+    (s.id IS NOT NULL) AS selector_present,\n+    COALESCE(s.enabled, false) AS selector_enabled,\n+    COALESCE(s.expires_at <= n.decision_at, false) AS selector_expired,\n+    (c.id IS NOT NULL) AS capability_present,\n+    c.capability_version,\n+    COALESCE(c.supports_route AND c.enabled AND (c.expires_at IS NULL OR c.expires_at > n.decision_at), false)\n+      AS capability_supports_route,\n+    COALESCE(c.supports_missing_selector AND c.enabled AND (c.expires_at IS NULL OR c.expires_at > n.decision_at), false)\n+      AS capability_supports_missing_selector,\n+    CASE\n+      WHEN n.worker_backend NOT IN ('wgp', 'vibecomfy') THEN false\n+      WHEN n.route_key IS NULL OR n.route_key = '' OR n.route_key ~ '\\s' THEN false\n+      WHEN c.id IS NULL THEN false\n+      WHEN s.id IS NULL THEN\n+        n.worker_backend = 'wgp'\n+        AND COALESCE(c.supports_missing_selector AND c.enabled AND (c.expires_at IS NULL OR c.expires_at > n.decision_at), false)\n+      ELSE\n+        s.enabled\n+        AND (s.expires_at IS NULL OR s.expires_at > n.decision_at)\n+        AND s.selected_backend = n.worker_backend\n+        AND COALESCE(c.supports_route AND c.enabled AND (c.expires_at IS NULL OR c.expires_at > n.decision_at), false)\n+    END AS eligible,\n+    CASE\n+      WHEN n.worker_backend NOT IN ('wgp', 'vibecomfy') THEN 'malformed_worker_backend'\n+      WHEN n.route_key IS NULL OR n.route_key = '' OR n.route_key ~ '\\s' THEN 'malformed_route_key'\n+      WHEN c.id IS NULL THEN 'missing_capability'\n+      WHEN s.id IS NULL AND n.worker_backend = 'vibecomfy' THEN 'missing_selector_vibecomfy_no_claim'\n+      WHEN s.id IS NULL AND NOT COALESCE(c.supports_missing_selector AND c.enabled AND (c.expires_at IS NULL OR c.expires_at > n.decision_at), false) THEN 'missing_selector_capability_unsupported'\n+      WHEN s.id IS NULL THEN 'missing_selector_wgp_capability_supported'\n+      WHEN NOT s.enabled THEN 'selector_disabled'\n+      WHEN s.expires_at IS NOT NULL AND s.expires_at <= n.decision_at THEN 'selector_expired'\n+      WHEN s.selected_backend <> n.worker_backend THEN 'backend_mismatch'\n+      WHEN NOT COALESCE(c.enabled, false) THEN 'capability_disabled'\n+      WHEN c.expires_at IS NOT NULL AND c.expires_at <= n.decision_at THEN 'capability_expired'\n+      WHEN NOT COALESCE(c.supports_route, false) THEN 'capability_unsupported'\n+      ELSE 'eligible'\n+    END AS decision_reason,\n+    CASE\n+      WHEN s.id IS NULL THEN NULL\n+      ELSE jsonb_build_object(\n+        'selector_namespace', s.selector_namespace,\n+        'route_key', s.route_key,\n+        'selected_backend', s.selected_backend,\n+        'selector_version', s.selector_version,\n+        'enabled', s.enabled,\n+        'expires_at', s.expires_at,\n+        'min_worker_version', s.min_worker_version\n+      )\n+    END AS selector_snapshot,\n+    CASE\n+      WHEN c.id IS NULL THEN NULL\n+      ELSE jsonb_build_object(\n+        'backend', c.backend,\n+        'route_key', c.route_key,\n+        'supports_route', c.supports_route,\n+        'supports_missing_selector', c.supports_missing_selector,\n+        'capability_version', c.capability_version,\n+        'enabled', c.enabled,\n+        'expires_at', c.expires_at,\n+        'min_worker_version', c.min_worker_version\n+      )\n+    END AS capability_snapshot\n+  FROM normalized n\n+  LEFT JOIN selector_row s ON true\n+  LEFT JOIN capability_row c ON true;\n+$$;\n+\n+REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.route_backend_claim_decision(text, text, text, timestamptz) FROM anon, authenticated;\n+GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.route_backend_claim_decision(text, text, text, timestamptz) TO service_role;\n+\n+INSERT INTO public.route_backend_capabilities (\n+  backend,\n+  route_key,\n+  supports_route,\n+  supports_missing_selector,\n+  capability_version,\n+  metadata\n+)\n+SELECT\n+  'wgp' AS backend,\n+  tt.name AS route_key,\n+  true AS supports_route,\n+  true AS supports_missing_selector,\n+  1 AS capability_version,\n+  jsonb_build_object(\n+    'seeded_by', '20260506110000_add_route_backend_selector_control_plane',\n+    'source', 'active_gpu_task_types',\n+    'task_type', tt.name\n+  ) AS metadata\n+FROM public.task_types tt\n+WHERE tt.is_active = true\n+  AND tt.run_type = 'gpu'\n+ON CONFLICT (backend, route_key) DO UPDATE\n+SET\n+  supports_route = EXCLUDED.supports_route,\n+  supports_missing_selector = EXCLUDED.supports_missing_selector,\n+  metadata = public.route_backend_capabilities.metadata || EXCLUDED.metadata,\n+  updated_at = now();\n+\n+COMMENT ON TABLE public.route_backend_selectors IS\n+  'Production backend selector allowlist keyed by selector namespace and canonical route key. Missing production rows are not an implicit VibeComfy fallback.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.route_backend_selectors.selector_namespace IS\n+  'Selector namespace, normally production. Allows bounded non-production selector maps without malformed namespace values.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.route_backend_selectors.route_key IS\n+  'Canonical route key produced by app/worker serializers. Direct routes use task type; dimensional routes include route dimensions.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.route_backend_selectors.selected_backend IS\n+  'Selected execution backend. Valid values are wgp and vibecomfy; comfy is intentionally not accepted as an alias.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.route_backend_selectors.selector_version IS\n+  'Monotonic selector version emitted in claim responses, task metadata, logs, and child route snapshots.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.route_backend_selectors.expires_at IS\n+  'Optional stale-entry guard. Expired selectors fail closed during claim decisions.';\n+\n+COMMENT ON TABLE public.route_backend_capabilities IS\n+  'SQL-side backend capability registry used by claim/count decisions. Missing rows mean no-claim by construction.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.route_backend_capabilities.supports_missing_selector IS\n+  'Allows WGP to claim a route when the selector row is missing. A check constraint forbids this for vibecomfy.';\n+COMMENT ON CONSTRAINT route_backend_capabilities_missing_selector_wgp_only_check ON public.route_backend_capabilities IS\n+  'VibeComfy must never support missing selector fallback; production missing selectors cannot imply VibeComfy.';\n+\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.tasks.selector_namespace IS\n+  'Create-time route selector namespace snapshot for observability and later child-row pinning.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.tasks.route_key IS\n+  'Create-time canonical route key snapshot. Claim authorization still uses live selector/capability data.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.tasks.selected_backend IS\n+  'Create-time selected backend snapshot for observability and child route pinning.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.tasks.selector_version IS\n+  'Create-time selector version snapshot. Claim-time selector version is persisted separately.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.tasks.route_selection_snapshot IS\n+  'Create-time route selection snapshot JSON, including support state needed by parent-created child rows.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.tasks.claimed_backend IS\n+  'Claim-time backend that actually claimed the task. Active accounting should use this persisted field.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.tasks.claimed_selector_namespace IS\n+  'Claim-time selector namespace used for the live decision.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.tasks.claimed_route_key IS\n+  'Claim-time canonical route key used for the live decision.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.tasks.claimed_selector_version IS\n+  'Claim-time selector version used for the live decision, visible in worker logs and task metadata.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.tasks.claimed_capability_version IS\n+  'Claim-time capability row version used for backend eligibility.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.tasks.claim_decision_reason IS\n+  'Claim-time reason such as eligible, missing_capability, backend_mismatch, selector_expired, or missing_selector_wgp_capability_supported.';\n+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.tasks.claim_decision_snapshot IS\n+  'Claim-time selector/capability snapshot for logs, debugging, rollback accounting, and fail-closed diagnostics.';\n+\n+COMMENT ON FUNCTION public.route_backend_claim_decision(text, text, text, timestamptz) IS\n+  'Evaluates live selector plus backend capability eligibility. Missing capability rows return eligible=false. Missing selector rows can only be claimed by WGP when capability explicitly supports missing-selector fallback.';\n+\n+COMMIT;\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
# Sprint 6: Production Selector and Claim Contract

## Overall Context

This sprint turns local selector ideas into production claim behavior before orchestrated routes depend on them. It is the control-plane foundation for rollback and canary.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Production missing selector key means WGP/no-claim, never implicit Comfy.
- Workers must not claim routes they cannot execute.
- Selector version and selected backend must be visible in logs or task metadata.
- Child rows created later must be able to snapshot route selection.

## Sprint Goal

Make selector and claim behavior concrete for production.

## Required Deliverables

- Selector schema/namespace.
- Route-key serialization, including direct variants where needed.
- Index/RPC/query behavior.
- Cache TTL and rollback SLO.
- Malformed/unauthorized/stale-entry tests.
- Claim-time backend eligibility or pre-execution requeue/fail-closed guard.
- Selector-version logging.
- Child-route snapshot field contract for later parent-created rows.

## Exit Criteria

Missing production route key means WGP/no-claim; mismatched workers cannot claim or execute selected routes; selector unreachable behavior and rollback SLO are tested; selected backend/selector version can be pinned for child rows created after parent claim.

    "Seed `route_backend_capabilities` conservatively; missing or unsupported capability rows must be treated as no-claim.",

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Idea:
# Sprint 6: Production Selector and Claim Contract

## Overall Context

This sprint turns local selector ideas into production claim behavior before orchestrated routes depend on them. It is the control-plane foundation for rollback and canary.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Production missing selector key means WGP/no-claim, never implicit Comfy.
- Workers must not claim routes they cannot execute.
- Selector version and selected backend must be visible in logs or task metadata.
- Child rows created later must be able to snapshot route selection.

## Sprint Goal

Make selector and claim behavior concrete for production.

## Required Deliverables

- Selector schema/namespace.
- Route-key serialization, including direct variants where needed.
- Index/RPC/query behavior.
- Cache TTL and rollback SLO.
- Malformed/unauthorized/stale-entry tests.
- Claim-time backend eligibility or pre-execution requeue/fail-closed guard.
- Selector-version logging.
- Child-route snapshot field contract for later parent-created rows.

## Exit Criteria

Missing production route key means WGP/no-claim; mismatched workers cannot claim or execute selected routes; selector unreachable behavior and rollback SLO are tested; selected backend/selector version can be pinned for child rows created after parent claim.

dimensional keys with Python slug parity\", () => {\n+    const params = {\n+      model_name: \"LTX2 Distilled 13B\",\n+      travel_guidance: { kind: \"LTX+Anchor\" },\n+      video_source: \"https://example.com/guide.mp4\",\n+      override_profile: 3,\n+    };\n+\n+    expect(deriveRouteKey(\"travel_segment\", params)).toBe(\n+ \"first_last\",\n+      }),\n+    ).toBe(\n+      \"individual_travel_segment__model-wan22_i2v__guidance-none__continuity-first_last__profile-default\",\n+    );\n+  });\n+\n+  it(\"derives Wan VACE join keys with default join guidance and continuity\", () => {\n+    expect(\n+      deriveRouteKey(\"join_clips_segment\", {\n+        model_name: \"Wan 2.2 VACE\",\n+      }),\n+    ).toBe(\n+      \"join_clips_segment__model-wan22_vace__guidance-vace__continuity-join_bridge__profile-default\",\n+    );\n+  });\n+\n+  it(\"serializes route snapshot fields for later child row pinning\", () => {\n+    expect(\n+      routeSnapshotFields({\n+        taskType: \"join_clips_segment\",\n+        params: { model_name: \"Wan 2.2 VACE\" },\n+        selectedBackend: \"wgp\",\n+        selectorVersion: 7,\n+        taskId: \"task-1\",\n+        [REDACTED],\n+      }),\n+    ).toEqual({\n+      selector_namespace: \"production\",\n+      [REDACTED],\n+      selected_backend: expect(parseRouteBackend(\"wgp\")).toBe(\"wgp\");\n+    expect(parseRouteBackend(\"vibecomfy\")).toBe(\"vibecomfy\");\n+    expect(() => parseRouteBackend(\"comfy\")).toThrow(\"Unsupported route backend\");\n+  });\n+\n+  it(\"matches Python slugging for pluses, punctuation, and empty values\", () => {\n+    expect(slugRoutePart(\"LTX+Anchor\")).toBe(\"ltx_plus_anchor\");\n+    expect(slugRoutePart(\" Wan 2.2 VACE \")).toBe(\"wan_2_2_vace\");\n+    expect(slugRoutePart(\"\")).toBe(\"none\");\n+  });\n+});\n*** End Patch\n"

        Idea:
# Sprint 6: Production Selector and Claim Contract

## Overall Context

This sprint turns local selector ideas into production claim behavior before orchestrated routes depend on them. It is the control-plane foundation for rollback and canary.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Production missing selector key means WGP/no-claim, never implicit Comfy.
- Workers must not claim routes they cannot execute.
- Selector version and selected backend must be visible in logs or task metadata.
- Child rows created later must be able to snapshot route selection.

## Sprint Goal

Make selector and claim behavior concrete for production.

## Required Deliverables

- Selector schema/namespace.
- Route-key serialization, including direct variants where needed.
- Index/RPC/query behavior.
- Cache TTL and rollback SLO.
- Malformed/unauthorized/stale-entry tests.
- Claim-time backend eligibility or pre-execution requeue/fail-closed guard.
- Selector-version logging.
- Child-route snapshot field contract for later parent-created rows.

## Exit Criteria

Missing production route key means WGP/no-claim; mismatched workers cannot claim or execute selected routes; selector unreachable behavior and rollback SLO are tested; selected backend/selector version can be pinned for child rows created after parent claim.

    "Production missing selector key means WGP/no-claim, never implicit Comfy or `vibecomfy`.",
    "Seed `route_backend_capabilities` conservatively; missing or unsupported capability rows must be treated as no-claim.",
    "Do not infer WGP support from `task_types.run_type` inside claim/count SQL; use the new SQL-side capability table.",
    "Queued eligibility uses live selector plus capability data; task snapshot columns are observability and child-pinning metadata, not rollback authorization.",
    "Active accounting must use persisted `claimed_*` fields so selector rollback does not undercount in-progress work.",
    "Selector namespace defaults to `production`; keep it configurable for non-production without accepting malformed namespace values.",
    "`vibecomfy` is the backend identifier; do not accept `comfy` as an alias.",
    "Banodoco worker-pool filtering remains separate from WGP/VibeComfy backend selector eligibility.",
    "Keep route helper modules lightweight; no imports of WGP, VibeComfy, or heavy runtime modules.",
    "Claim-path selector cache SLO is zero: each queued claim decision should hit live DB state through the RPC. Any create-task cache must be absent or bounded at `ROUTE_SELECTOR_CACHE_TTL_MS <= 30000` and tested.",

    "Every `add_task_to_db` caller needs an explicit route snapshot or explicit WGP-safe/null decision.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the direct adapter still has a correctness risk around resolution parity. current vibecomfy ready templates hardcode image dimensions (`image/z_image` uses `emptysd3latentimage` 1024x1024 and `image/qwen_image_2512` uses 768x768), while `vibecomfy run` exposes no `--resolution` flag; the revised plan says a temporary scratchpad/input file may be generated only if supported, otherwise resolution is left out. that means a vibecomfy-supported direct route can silently ignore the worker's `resolution` param unless scratchpad resolution patching is made mandatory for supported direct routes or the support is explicitly limited to default-resolution smoke fixtures. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: the attached success criteria are stale and do not match the current plan body. they still require 'no `usestate` for `activepairdata`', '`setactivepairdata` fully removed', 'sync effect deleted', and '`onpairclick` simplified to `(pairindex: number) => void`', which are the opposite of the current targeted-sync plan. as presented, the criteria are not usable for review or execution. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue. 6: enforce live selector/capability eligibility during service-role claims.\n+\n+BEGIN;\n+\n+DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.claim_next_task_service_role(TEXT, BOOLEAN, TEXT, BOOLEAN, INT);\n+\n+CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.claim_next_task_service_role(\n+  p_worker_id TEXT,\n+  p_include_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,\n+  p_run_type TEXT DEFAULT NULL,\n+  p_same_model_only BOOLEAN DEFAULT v_claim_decision_snapshot JSONB;\n+  v_status_filter task_status[];\n+  v_worker_model TEXT;\n+  v_has_starving_task BOOLEAN := FALSE;\n+  v_no_matching_tasks BOOLEAN := FALSE;\n+  v_effective_max_task_wait_minutes INT := COALESCE(p_max_task_wait_minutes, 5);\n+  v_effective_worker_backend TEXT := COALESCE(NULLIF(p_worker_backend, ''), 'wgp');\n+  v_effective_selector_namespace TEXT := COALESCE(NULLIF(p_selector_namespace, ''), 'production');\n+BEGIN\n+  IF v_effective_worker_backend NOT IN ('wgp', 'vibecomfy') THEN\n+    RETURN;\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  IF v_effective_selector_namespace !~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}$' THEN\n+    RETURN;\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  IF p_include_active THEN\n+    v_status_filter := ARRAY['Queued'::task_status, 'In Progress'::task_status];\n+  ELSE\n+    v_status_filter := ARRAY['Queued'::task_status];\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  SELECT current_model INTO v_worker_model\n+  FROM workers\n+  WHERE id = p_worker_id AND status = 'active';\n+\n+  CREATE TEMP TABLE _eligible_users ON COMMIT DROP AS\n+    SELECT\n+      u.id as user_id,\n+      u.credits,\n+      COALESCE((u.settings->'ui'->'generationMethods'->>'inCloud')::boolean, true) as allows_cloud,\n+      COUNT(in_progress_tasks.id) as in_progress_count\n+    FROM users u\n+    LEFT JOIN projects p ON p.user_id = u.id\n+    LEFT JOIN tasks in_progress_tasks ON in_progress_tasks.project_id = p.id\n+      AND in_progress_tasks.status = 'In Progress'::task_status\n+      AND COALESCE(in_progress_tasks.task_type, '') NOT ILIKE '%orchestrator%'\n+    WHERE u.credits > 0\n+      AND COALESCE((u.settings->'ui'->'generationMethods'->>'inCloud')::boolean, true) = true\n+    GROUP BY u.id, u.credits, u.settings\n+    HAVING COUNT(in_progress_tasks.id) < 5;\n+\n+  IF p_same_model_only AND v_worker_model IS NOT NULL THEN\n+    SELECT\n+      NOT bool_or(get_task_model(t.params) = v_worker_model),\n+      bool_or(t.created_at < NOW() - (v_effective_max_task_wait_minutes || ' minutes')::interval)\n+    INTO v_no_matching_tasks, v_has_starving_task\n+    FROM tasks t\n+    JOIN projects p ON t.project_id = p.id\n+    JOIN LATERAL public.route_backend_claim_decision(\n+      v_effective_selector_namespace,\n+      t.route_key,\n+      v_effective_worker_backend,\n+      now()\n+    ) rd ON rd.eligible\n+    WHERE t.status = 'Queued'::task_status\n+      AND all_dependencies_complete(t.dependant_on)\n+      AND EXISTS (\n+        SELECT 1 FROM _eligible_users eu WHERE eu.user_id = p.user_id\n+      )\n+      AND (\n+        p_run_type IS NULL OR\n+        get_task_run_type(t.task_type) = p_run_type\n+      );\n+\n+    v_no_matching_tasks := COALESCE(v_no_matching_tasks, TRUE);\n+    v_has_starving_task := COALESCE(v_has_starving_task, FALSE);\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  WITH ready_tasks AS (\n+    SELECT\n+      t.id,\n+      t.params,\n+      t.task_type,\n+      t.project_id,\n+      t.created_at,\n+      t.selector_namespace,\n+      t.route_key,\n+      t.selected_backend,\n+      t.selector_version,\n+      t.route_selection_snapshot,\n+      p.user_id,\n+      rd.worker_backend AS decision_worker_backend,\n+      rd.selector_namespace AS decision_selector_namespace,\n+      rd.route_key AS decision_route_key,\n+      rd.selector_version AS decision_selector_version,\n+      rd.capability_version AS decision_capability_version,\n+      rd.decision_reason,\n+      jsonb_build_object(\n+ 'selected_backend', rd.selected_backend,\n+        'selector_version', rd.selector_version,\n+        'selector_present', rd.selector_present,\n+        'selector_enabled', rd.selector_enabled,\n+        'selector_expired', rd.selector_expired,\n+        'capability_present', rd.capability_present,\n+        'capability_version', rd.capability_version,\n+        'capability_supports_route', rd.capability_supports_route,\n+        'capability_supports_missing_selector', rd.capability_supports_missing_selector,\n+        'selector_snapshot', rd.selector_snapshot,\n+        'capability_snapshot', rd.capability_snapshot,\n+        'task_snapshot', jsonb_build_object(\n+          'selector_namespace', t.selector_namespace,\n+          'route_key', t.route_key,\n+          'selected_backend', t.selected_backend,\n+          'selector_version', t.selector_version,\n+          'route_selection_snapshot', t.route_selection_snapshot\n+        )\n+      ) AS decision_snapshot,\n+      ROW_NUMBER() OVER (\n+        ORDER BY\n+          CASE\n+            WHEN v_has_starving_task OR v_no_matching_tasks THEN NULL\n+            WHEN v_worker_model IS NOT NULL\n+                 AND get_task_model(t.params) = v_worker_model\n+            THEN 0\n+            ELSE 1\n+          END NULLS LAST,\n+          t.created_at ASC\n+      ) as rn\n+    FROM tasks t\n+    JOIN projects p ON t.project_id = p.id\n+    JOIN LATERAL public.route_backend_claim_decision(\n+      v_effective_selector_namespace,\n+      t.route_key,\n+      v_effective_worker_backend,\n+      now()\n+    ) rd ON rd.eligible\n+    WHERE t.status = 'Queued'::task_status\n+      AND all_dependencies_complete(t.dependant_on)\n+      AND EXISTS (\n+        SELECT 1 FROM _eligible_users eu WHERE eu.user_id = p.user_id\n+      )\n+      AND (\n+        p_run_type IS NULL OR\n+        get_task_run_type(t.task_type) = p_run_type\n+      )\n+      AND (\n+ )\n+  UPDATE tasks\n+  SET\n+    status = CASE\n+      WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN 'In Progress'::task_status\n+      ELSE status\n+    END,\n+    worker_id = CASE\n+      WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN p_worker_id\n+      ELSE worker_id\n+    END,\n+    updated_at = CASE\n+      WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN NOW()\n+      ELSE updated_at\n+    END,\n+    generation_started_at = CASE\n+      WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN NOW()\n+      ELSE generation_started_at\n+    END,\n+    claimed_backend = CASE\n+      WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN rt.decision_worker_backend\n+      ELSE claimed_backend\n+    END,\n+    claimed_selector_namespace = CASE\n+      WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN rt.decision_selector_namespace\n+      ELSE claimed_selector_namespace\n+    END,\n+    claimed_route_key = CASE\n+      WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN rt.decision_route_key\n+      ELSE claimed_route_key\n+    END,\n+    claimed_selector_version = CASE\n+      WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN rt.decision_selector_version\n+      ELSE claimed_selector_version\n+    END,\n+    claimed_capability_version = CASE\n+      WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN rt.decision_capability_version\n+      ELSE claimed_capability_version\n+    END,\n+    claim_decision_reason = CASE\n+      WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN rt.decision_reason\n+      ELSE claim_decision_reason\n+    END,\n+    claim_decision_snapshot = CASE\n+      WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status v_task_type,\n+    v_project_id,\n+    v_user_id,\n+    v_selector_namespace,\n+    v_route_key,\n+    v_selected_backend,\n+    v_selector_version,\n+    v_route_selection_snapshot,\n+    v_claimed_backend,\n+    v_claimed_selector_namespace,\n+    v_claimed_route_key,\n+    v_claimed_selector_version,\n+    v_claimed_capability_version,\n+    v_claim_decision_reason,\n+    v_claim_decision_snapshot;\n+\n+  IF v_task_id IS NOT NULL THEN\n+    task_id := v_task_id;\n+    params := v_params;\n+    task_type := v_task_type;\n+    project_id := v_project_id;\n+    user_id := v_user_id;\n+    selector_namespace := v_selector_namespace;\n+    route_key := v_route_key;\n+    selected_backend := v_selected_backend;\n+    selector_version := v_selector_version;\n+    route_selection_snapshot := v_route_selection_snapshot;\n+    claimed_backend := v_claimed_backend;\n+    claimed_selector_namespace := v_claimed_selector_namespace;\n+    claimed_route_key := v_claimed_route_key;\n+    claimed_selector_version := v_claimed_selector_version;\n+    claimed_capability_version := v_claimed_capability_version;\n+    claim_decision_reason := v_claim_decision_reason;\n+    claim_decision_snapshot := v_claim_decision_snapshot;\n+    RETURN NEXT;\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  RETURN;\n+END;\n+$$;\n+\n+COMMENT ON FUNCTION public.claim_next_task_service_role(TEXT, BOOLEAN, TEXT, BOOLEAN, INT, TEXT, TEXT) IS\n+'Claims next eligible task for service role using live route_backend_selectors plus route_backend_capabilities. Missing capability rows mean no-claim; missing selector rows can only be claimed by WGP when capability explicitly supports missing-selector fallback.         Idea:
# Sprint 6: Production Selector and Claim Contract

## Overall Context

This sprint turns local selector ideas into production claim behavior before orchestrated routes depend on them. It is the control-plane foundation for rollback and canary.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Production missing selector key means WGP/no-claim, never implicit Comfy.
- Workers must not claim routes they cannot execute.
- Selector version and selected backend must be visible in logs or task metadata.
- Child rows created later must be able to snapshot route selection.

## Sprint Goal

Make selector and claim behavior concrete for production.

## Required Deliverables

- Selector schema/namespace.
- Route-key serialization, including direct variants where needed.
- Index/RPC/query behavior.
- Cache TTL and rollback SLO.
- Malformed/unauthorized/stale-entry tests.
- Claim-time backend eligibility or pre-execution requeue/fail-closed guard.
- Selector-version logging.
- Child-route snapshot field contract for later parent-created rows.

## Exit Criteria

Missing production route key means WGP/no-claim; mismatched workers cannot claim or execute selected routes; selector unreachable behavior and rollback SLO are tested; selected backend/selector version can be pinned for child rows created after parent claim.

    "Production missing selector key means WGP/no-claim, never implicit Comfy or `vibecomfy`.",
    "Seed `route_backend_capabilities` conservatively; missing or unsupported capability rows must be treated as no-claim.",
    "Do not infer WGP support from `task_types.run_type` inside claim/count SQL; use the new SQL-side capability table.",
    "Queued eligibility uses live selector plus capability data; task snapshot columns are observability and child-pinning metadata, not rollback authorization.",
    "Active accounting must use persisted `claimed_*` fields so selector rollback does not undercount in-progress work.",
    "Selector namespace defaults to `production`; keep it configurable for non-production without accepting malformed namespace values.",
    "`vibecomfy` is the backend identifier; do not accept `comfy` as an alias.",
    "Banodoco worker-pool filtering remains separate from WGP/VibeComfy backend selector eligibility.",
    "Keep route helper modules lightweight; no imports of WGP, VibeComfy, or heavy runtime modules.",
    "Claim-path selector cache SLO is zero: each queued claim decision should hit live DB state through the RPC. Any create-task cache must be absent or bounded at `ROUTE_SELECTOR_CACHE_TTL_MS <= 30000` and tested.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app-cloud-chain/supabase/migrations/20260506114000_update_task_counts_route_selector.sql\n+-- Sprint 6: align service-role task counts with live claim eligibility.\n+\n+BEGIN;\n+\n+DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.count_eligible_tasks_service_role(BOOLEAN, TEXT);\n+DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.count_queued_tasks_breakdown_service_role(TEXT);\n+\n+CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.count_eligible_tasks_service_role(\n+  p_include_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,\n+  p_run_type TEXT DEFAULT NULL,\n+  p_worker_backend TEXT DEFAULT 'wgp',\n+  p_selector_namespace TEXT DEFAULT 'production'\n+)\n+RETURNS INTEGER\n+LANGUAGE plpgsql\n+STABLE\n+SECURITY DEFINER\n+AS $$\n+DECLARE\n+  v_total_capacity INTEGER := 0;\n+  v_effective_worker_backend TEXT := COALESCE(NULLIF(p_worker_backend, ''), 'wgp');\n+  v_effective_selector_namespace TEXT := COALESCE(NULLIF(p_selector_namespace, ''), 'production');\n+BEGIN\n+  IF v_effective_worker_backend NOT IN ('wgp', 'vibecomfy') THEN\n+    RETURN 0;\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  IF v_effective_selector_namespace !~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}$' THEN\n+    RETURN 0;\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  WITH per_user_capacity AS (\n+    SELECT\n+      u.id AS user_id,\n+      COUNT(CASE\n+        WHEN t.status = 'In Progress'::task_status\n+          AND COALESCE(t.task_type, '') NOT ILIKE '%orchestrator%'\n+        THEN 1\n+      END) AS in_progress_count,\n+      COUNT(CASE\n+        WHEN t.status = 'In Progress'::task_status\n+          AND COALESCE(t.task_type, '') NOT ILIKE '%orchestrator%'\n+          AND t.claimed_backend = v_effective_worker_backend\n+          AND t.claimed_selector_namespace = v_effective_selector_namespace\n+          AND (\n+            p_run_type IS NULL\n+            OR get_task_run_type(t.task_type) = p_run_type\n+          )\n+        THEN 1\n+      END) AS active_backend_count,\n+      COUNT(CASE\n+        WHEN t.status = 'Queued'::task_status\n+          AND all_dependencies_complete(t.dependant_on)\n+          AND (\n+            p_run_type IS NULL\n+            OR get_task_run_type(t.task_type) = p_run_type\n+          )\n+          AND EXISTS (\n+            SELECT 1\n+            FROM public.route_backend_claim_decision(\n+              v_effective_selector_namespace,\n+              t.route_key,\n+              v_effective_worker_backend,\n+              now()\n+            ) rd\n+            WHERE rd.eligible = true\n+          )\n+        THEN 1\n+      END) AS ready_queued_count\n+    FROM users u\n+    LEFT JOIN projects p ON p.user_id = u.id\n+    LEFT JOIN tasks t ON t.project_id = p.id\n+    WHERE u.credits > 0\n+      AND COALESCE((u.settings->'ui'->'generationMethods'->>'inCloud')::boolean, true) = true\n+    GROUP BY u.id, u.credits, u.settings\n+  )\n+  SELECT COALESCE(SUM(\n+    CASE\n+      WHEN p_include_active THEN\n+        active_backend_count + GREATEST(0, LEAST(5 - in_progress_count, ready_queued_count))\n+      ELSE\n+        GREATEST(0, LEAST(5 - in_progress_count, ready_queued_count))\n+    END\n+  ), 0) INTO v_total_capacity\n+  FROM per_user_capacity;\n+\n+  RETURN v_total_capacity;\n+END;\n+$$;\n+\n+CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.count_queued_tasks_breakdown_service_role(\n+  p_run_type TEXT DEFAULT NULL,\n+  p_worker_backend TEXT DEFAULT 'wgp',\n+  p_selector_namespace TEXT DEFAULT 'production'\n+)\n+RETURNS TABLE(\n+  claimable_now INTEGER,\n+  blocked_by_capacity INTEGER,\n+  blocked_by_deps INTEGER,\n+  blocked_by_settings INTEGER,\n+  total_queued INTEGER\n+)\n+LANGUAGE plpgsql\n+STABLE\n+SECURITY DEFINER\n+AS $$\n+DECLARE\n+  v_effective_worker_backend TEXT := COALESCE(NULLIF(p_worker_backend, ''), 'wgp');\n+  v_effective_selector_namespace TEXT := COALESCE(NULLIF(p_selector_namespace, ''), 'production');\n+BEGIN\n+  IF v_effective_worker_backend NOT IN ('wgp', 'vibecomfy')\n+     OR v_effective_selector_namespace !~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}$' THEN\n+    RETURN QUERY SELECT 0, 0, 0, 0, 0;\n+    RETURN;\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  RETURN QUERY\n+  WITH user_capacity AS (\n+    SELECT\n+      u.id AS user_id,\n+      u.credits,\n+      COALESCE((u.settings->'ui'->'generationMethods'->>'inCloud')::boolean, true) AS allows_cloud,\n+      COUNT(t.id) FILTER (\n+        WHERE t.status = 'In Progress'::task_status\n+        AND COALESCE(t.task_type, '') NOT ILIKE '%orchestrator%'\n+      ) AS in_progress_count\n+    FROM users u\n+    LEFT JOIN projects p ON p.user_id = u.id\n+    LEFT JOIN tasks t ON t.project_id = p.id\n+    WHERE u.credits > 0\n+    GROUP BY u.id, u.credits, u.settings\n+  ),\n+  categorized_tasks AS (\n+    SELECT\n+      t.id AS task_id,\n+      uc.user_id,\n+      uc.credits,\n+      uc.allows_cloud,\n+      uc.in_progress_count,\n+      all_dependencies_complete(t.dependant_on) AS deps_complete,\n+      rd.eligible AS route_eligible,\n+      CASE\n+        WHEN uc.credits IS NULL OR uc.credits <= 0 THEN 'excluded'\n+        WHEN NOT uc.allows_cloud THEN 'blocked_by_settings'\n+        WHEN NOT all_dependencies_complete(t.dependant_on) THEN 'blocked_by_deps'\n+        WHEN uc.in_progress_count >= 5 THEN 'blocked_by_capacity'\n+        WHEN COALESCE(rd.eligible, false) = false THEN 'blocked_by_settings'\n+        ELSE 'claimable_now'\n+      END AS category\n+    FROM tasks t\n+    JOIN projects p ON t.project_id = p.id\n+    LEFT JOIN user_capacity uc ON uc.user_id = p.user_id\n+    LEFT JOIN LATERAL public.route_backend_claim_decision(\n+      v_effective_selector_namespace,\n+      t.route_key,\n+      v_effective_worker_backend,\n+      now()\n+    ) rd ON true\n+    WHERE t.status = 'Queued'::task_status\n+      AND COALESCE(t.task_type, '') NOT ILIKE '%orchestrator%'\n+      AND (\n+        p_run_type IS NULL\n+        OR get_task_run_type(t.task_type) = p_run_type\n+      )\n+  )\n+  SELECT\n+    COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE category = 'claimable_now')::INTEGER AS claimable_now,\n+    COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE category = 'blocked_by_capacity')::INTEGER AS blocked_by_capacity,\n+    COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE category = 'blocked_by_deps')::INTEGER AS blocked_by_deps,\n+    COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE category = 'blocked_by_settings')::INTEGER AS blocked_by_settings,\n+    COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE category != 'excluded')::INTEGER AS total_queued\n+  FROM categorized_tasks;\n+END;\n+$$;\n+\n+COMMENT ON FUNCTION public.count_eligible_tasks_service_role(BOOLEAN, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT) IS\n+'Counts service-role claimable workload for a backend and selector namespace. Queued tasks use live selector/capability eligibility; active tasks use persisted claimed_* fields.';\n+\n+COMMENT ON FUNCTION public.count_queued_tasks_breakdown_service_role(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT) IS\n+'Breaks down queued tasks using the same live selector/capability eligibility as service-role claim for the requested backend and selector namespace.';\n+\n+COMMIT;\n*** End Patch\n"
}

null\n+      : parseRouteBackend(params.selected_backend);\n+    const selectorVersion = optionalSelectorVersion(params.selector_version);\n+    const routeSelectionSnapshot = optionalRouteSelectionSnapshot(params.route_selection_snapshot);\n \n     return {\n       tasks: [{\n         ...(workerTaskId ? { id: workerTaskId } : {}),\n         project_id: context.projectId,\n         task_type: taskType,\n         params,\n+        ...(routeKey ? { route_key: routeKey } : {}),\n+        ...(selectedBackend ? { selected_backend: selectedBackend } : {}),\n+        ...(selectorVersion !== null ? { selector_version: selectorVersion } : {}),\n+        ...(routeSelectionSnapshot ? { route_selection_snapshot: routeSelectionSnapshot } : {}),\n         status: \"Queued\",\n         created_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n         dependant_on: dependantOn,\n       }],\n     };\n*** End Patch\n"

        Idea:
# Sprint 6: Production Selector and Claim Contract

## Overall Context

This sprint turns local selector ideas into production claim behavior before orchestrated routes depend on them. It is the control-plane foundation for rollback and canary.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Production missing selector key means WGP/no-claim, never implicit Comfy.
- Workers must not claim routes they cannot execute.
- Selector version and selected backend must be visible in logs or task metadata.
- Child rows created later must be able to snapshot route selection.

## Sprint Goal

Make selector and claim behavior concrete for production.

## Required Deliverables

- Selector schema/namespace.
- Route-key serialization, including direct variants where needed.
- Index/RPC/query behavior.
- Cache TTL and rollback SLO.
- Malformed/unauthorized/stale-entry tests.
- Claim-time backend eligibility or pre-execution requeue/fail-closed guard.
- Selector-version logging.
- Child-route snapshot field contract for later parent-created rows.

## Exit Criteria

Missing production route key means WGP/no-claim; mismatched workers cannot claim or execute selected routes; selector unreachable behavior and rollback SLO are tested; selected backend/selector version can be pinned for child rows created after parent claim.

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Idea:
# Sprint 6: Production Selector and Claim Contract

## Overall Context

This sprint turns local selector ideas into production claim behavior before orchestrated routes depend on them. It is the control-plane foundation for rollback and canary.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Production missing selector key means WGP/no-claim, never implicit Comfy.
- Workers must not claim routes they cannot execute.
- Selector version and selected backend must be visible in logs or task metadata.
- Child rows created later must be able to snapshot route selection.

## Sprint Goal

Make selector and claim behavior concrete for production.

## Required Deliverables

- Selector schema/namespace.
- Route-key serialization, including direct variants where needed.
- Index/RPC/query behavior.
- Cache TTL and rollback SLO.
- Malformed/unauthorized/stale-entry tests.
- Claim-time backend eligibility or pre-execution requeue/fail-closed guard.
- Selector-version logging.
- Child-route snapshot field contract for later parent-created rows.

## Exit Criteria

Missing production route key means WGP/no-claim; mismatched workers cannot claim or execute selected routes; selector unreachable behavior and rollback SLO are tested; selected backend/selector version can be pinned for child rows created after parent claim.

(flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue. \"eligible\" : \"selector_backend_mismatch\",\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+assert.match(selectorSql, /missing_selector_vibecomfy_no_claim/);\n+assert.match(selectorSql, /missing_capability/);\n+assert.match(selectorSql, /supports_missing_selector = false OR backend = 'wgp'/);\n+assert.match(countsSql, /t\\.claimed_backend = v_effective_worker_backend/);\n+assert.match(countsSql, /t\\.claimed_selector_namespace = v_effective_selector_namespace/);\n+\n+assert.deepEqual(\n+  decide({\n+    selector: null,\n+    capability: { backend: \"wgp\", supports_route: true, supports_missing_selector: true },\n+    workerBackend: \"vibecomfy\",\n+  }),\n+  { eligible: false, reason: \"missing_selector_vibecomfy_no_claim\" },\n+);\n+\n+assert.equal(\n+  decide({\n+    selector: null,\n+    capability: { backend: \"wgp\", supports_route: true, supports_missing_selector: false },\n+    workerBackend: \"wgp\",\n+  }).eligible,\n+  false,\n+);\n+\n+assert.equal(\n+  decide({\n+    selector: null,\n+    capability: { backend: \"wgp\", supports_route: true, supports_missing_selector: true },\n+    workerBackend: \"wgp\",\n+  }).eligible,\n+  true,\n+);\n+\n+const activeTask = { claimed_backend: \"vibecomfy\", claimed_selector_namespace: \"production\" };\n+const liveRollbackDecision = decide({\n+  selector: { selected_backend: \"wgp\" },\n+  capability: { backend: \"wgp\", supports_route: true, supports_missing_selector: false },\n+  workerBackend: \"vibecomfy\",\n+});\n+assert.equal(liveRollbackDecision.eligible, false);\n+assert.equal(activeTask.claimed_backend, \"vibecomfy\");\n+assert.equal(activeTask.claimed_selector_namespace, \"production\");\n+\n+console.log(\"sprint6 contract ok\");\n*** End Patch\n"
}
